⚙️ The Only Way to Stop It

Awareness + Interruption.
The PFC must notice the impulse before it takes over — a microsecond window.

Pause and breathe.
This gives time for oxygen and blood flow to shift back from limbic areas to the PFC.

Label the impulse.
(“This is anger.” / “This is craving.”)
The act of labeling activates PFC control networks.

Redirect energy.
Instead of suppressing, transmute the impulse into controlled action (workout, focus, creation). ^KHsGZxKP

✅ 1. Survival Limbic Circuit → Addictions, Cravings

This includes:

Hypothalamus → hunger, thirst, sex drive

Ventral Striatum / Nucleus Accumbens → dopamine reward

Amygdala (fear/impulse) → “I want it NOW”

This circuit gives:

Overeating

Porn/sex cravings

Smoking, addictions

Impulsive behavior

These feelings are raw, fast, survival-based.

They feel like:

“I need this right now.”

This is NOT “heart.”
This is survival. ^qk9WlL8X

✅ 2. Higher Emotional Limbic Circuit → Heart, Purpose, Creativity

This is very different.

It uses:

Anterior Cingulate Cortex (ACC) → meaning, empathy, moral emotion

Mid-Cingulate Cortex (MCC) → drive, purpose, controlled emotion

Hippocampus → memories + imagination

Ventromedial PFC + Limbic link → emotional intuition (“gut feeling”)

This produces:

Creativity

Flow state

Love

Purpose

Inner calling

Compassion

Heart-based feelings

The feeling of “I’m meant to do this”

This is not survival.
This is meaning.

This is what people spiritually call:

Heart

Soul pull

Inner truth

Intuition ^4X2IaFe3

🟠 Old Limbic Layer → Survival

Animals have this.
It keeps the body alive.

🔵 New Limbic Layer → Emotion + Meaning

Humans developed this.
This is where creativity and “heart feeling” come from.

So addiction and creativity come from two different circuits inside the same larger system. ^n0PI8yA5

if you cant sit and study  for 2 hours try sitting more

How to repeat something everyday
1st - There should be something new that you are going to try today (if learning example)

Formula =   material +  method (one of this should be new in every repetition for not to make the repetition feel like hell)

day 1 = Learning new topic  + (Read + Process + Understand + take notes) 
day 2 = Learning same topic + ( creating Mind Maps)
day 3 = Learning same topic + ( Using Visualizaing concept in 3d in mind by  mapping concepts to prior Knowlege) <- This method clicked 
day 4 = Learning new topic + ( Using Visualizaing concept in 3d in mind)

Black Box
Counter: break problem into micro-steps, visualize solutions. ^k6xnx2tG

Boredom / Low stimulation → Limbic seeks easy dopamine.
Counter: pre-plan structured activities, engage mind actively, use low-dopamine tasks consciously. ^AFQGtJP1

Stress / Fatigue → PFC loses control, limbic dominates.
Counter: rest strategically, breath control, short physical resets. ^Lvdg1Svt

High Temptation Availability → cues pull limbic automatically.
Counter: remove temptation, create friction, environment control. ^SxkRF5iZ

 Turn Imaginations in to Real-time  3D Visualisations
1. It’s not just coding — it’s creativity + performance
2. convert imagination → pixels → animation → interaction.
3. It works directly with the GPU (rare in most CS jobs)
4. You simulate real-world behavior
5. You build experiences, not just software
6. It merges multiple fields
Computer Graphics
Mathematics
Physics
Art & Design
Animation
Human Perception
GPU Programming

✔ More observant
✔ More scientific
✔ More imaginative
✔ More connected to nature ^dQVplB7D

🔥 1. Companies need realistic simulations more than ever

Real-time graphics is used for:

Automotive design (Tesla, BMW, Porsche)

Architecture & real-estate

Flight simulators

Medical visualization

Robotics simulation

Digital twins of factories

Weather and climate simulation

Scientific research

VR/AR products (Meta, Apple Vision Pro)

Game engines (Unity, Unreal)

These are mission-critical industries.

It’s not “art” — it’s engineering + visualization.

🔥 2. Almost every industry is moving toward 3D

15 years ago, websites were simple.
Now:

Apple, Adidas, Nike, Marvel all use 3D interactive websites.

E-commerce uses 3D product viewers.

Maps are 3D.

Phones support AR.

Simulation-based AI training needs 3D environments.

Data visualization is becoming 3D and immersive.

3D is becoming a default expectation.

🔥 3. AI needs simulated worlds — graphics engineers build them

Large companies now train AI agents inside simulated 3D environments:

OpenAI uses simulation to train robot arms

Google DeepMind uses physics-based 3D environments

Tesla trains autopilot on simulated roads

Amazon trains robots in virtual warehouses

These simulations require:

physics

fluids

lighting

materials

collision

particle systems

Exactly what you are learning.

🔥 4. Entertainment is a trillion-dollar industry

Your skills directly apply to:

Games

VFX

Animated films

Virtual production (LED stages, Mandalorian tech)

3D advertisements

Music videos

Cinematics

This market is bigger than traditional software.

🔥 5. Real-time 3D is merging into UI/UX

New trend: interfaces with depth, motion, and spatial elements.

Companies need people who can:

design motion systems

visualize data

create interactions

optimize GPU performance

These roles are hard to fill.

🔥 6. Very few people know this field → HIGH DEMAND

Everyone learns:

React

Node

Python

Backend

App development

But very few learn:

GLSL

WebGL

Render pipelines

Shaders

Physics simulation

GPU optimization

This gives you a huge competitive advantage.

⭐ So yes — imagination is involved

But the solutions are used in real, serious, functional industries.

Imagine 3 sectors:

1. ARTISTIC problems

(You solve these)

particles

waves

animations

stylized visuals

2. SCIENTIFIC problems

(You solve these too)

fluid simulation

cloth simulation

lighting physics

real-world motion

3. INDUSTRIAL problems

(Companies pay high for this)

digital twins

robot simulation

architecture visualization

product prototyping

VR training ^BNzvpwZS

What I have Gained
- Learning
    - I can understand now
    - I can retain what I have understood
    - I can see it in my mind
    - I don't feel like why am I even doing it
    - I don't feel like I am being dragged while learning
- Creativity
    - I think 0.001% of creativity has unlocked
    - I can feel how creativity feels
- Emotional Control
    - Disconnect
        - I can disconnect from anything at anytime.
        - I can stop feeling bad emotions which will cause me illusionary suffering
    - Connect
        - I can connect to what ever task I am doing  and visualise it in my mind.
        - I can see where i need to put more effort. 
- Separation From World
        -  I can see that world is completely a separate thing  from me.
        -  when I am doing my task even my body is part of that separate world
        -  Feels Like I am just a Function operating with no defined rules to complete 
            that function.
        - Even though I cant be in that state far long because there are still some other
           thoughts from body and other part keep pulling me away
